# Run 2 UL dataset configs: research report

Generated 2026-10-04 by the config-research agent. Nothing under the repo was modified; all drafts are in this directory (`configs_run2/`).

## 0. Summary of decisions

- **Source of truth for dataset existence:** DBS REST API (`https://cmsweb.cern.ch/dbs/prod/global/DBSReader/datasets?dataset=<wildcard>&dataset_access_type=VALID`) with the user's grid proxy; `filesummaries` for num_file/num_event/file_size; `runs` for data run ranges. All numbers below were queried today.
- **Campaign filter (MC):** only *standard-condition* NanoAODv9 datasets are used, i.e. the conditions string must match exactly `RunIISummer20UL16NanoAODAPVv9-106X_mcRun2_asymptotic_preVFP_v11[_extN]-vN`; `RunIISummer20UL16NanoAODv9-106X_mcRun2_asymptotic_v17[_extN]-vN`; `RunIISummer20UL17NanoAODv9-106X_mc2017_realistic_v9[_extN]-vN`; `RunIISummer20UL18NanoAODv9-106X_upgrade2018_realistic_v16_L1v1[_extN]-vN`. Variants with `JMENano`, `FlatPU0to75`, `FSUL1x` (FastSim), `EpsilonPU`/`PUMu4` (2017H), `PUForTRK`, `PUForMUOVal`, `Pilot`, `HerwigJetPartonBugFix`, and the older `NanoAODv2`/`NanoAODAPVv2` are excluded.
- **Global tags:** the DBS campaign strings carry the production GTs `106X_mcRun2_asymptotic_preVFP_v11` (UL16APV), `106X_mcRun2_asymptotic_v17` (UL16), `106X_mc2017_realistic_v9` (UL17) and `106X_upgrade2018_realistic_v16_L1v1` (UL18). The PdmV guidance quotes `106X_mc2017_realistic_v10` for UL17, but **no UL17 NanoAODv9 or MiniAODv2 dataset with `_v10` exists on DBS** (checked `/*/RunIISummer20UL17NanoAODv9-106X_mc2017_realistic_v10*/NANOAODSIM` and the MiniAODv2 equivalent: 0 hits); v10 must be the analysis-time conditions GT, the files were produced with v9. Data: the standard NanoAODv9 was made with `106X_dataRun2_v35`, the 15Feb2022 re-reco NanoAOD with `_v36` (hence `GT36`); the PdmV-quoted `106X_dataRun2_v37` is again the analysis-time GT, not a DBS string.
- **Version rule:** for each primary dataset and extension tag (no tag, `_ext1`, `_ext2`) the highest `-vN` is taken; `_extN` datasets are separate config entries with identical xsec and `dataset` = primary name + `_extN` (so that output names do not collide). Other VALID versions are listed in section 6.
- **Data choice:** where the 15Feb2022 ReReReco (high-pT muon fix) exists - SingleMuon 2017B-F, SingleMuon 2018A-D, EGamma 2018A-D - its NanoAODv9 (`*_NanoAODv9_GT36-vN`) is put in the config and the standard dataset is kept as the alternative, except SingleMuon 2017B whose re-reco is missing 25% of the certified lumi sections (standard kept); 2016 and the 2017 SingleElectron/SinglePhoton PDs have no re-reco (section 1.0).
- **Data campaign strings verified on DBS:** UL16APV: `Run2016{B-ver1,B-ver2,C,D,E,F}-HIPM_UL2016_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9-vN`; UL16: `Run2016{F,G,H}-UL2016_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9-vN`; UL17: `Run2017{B,C,D,E,F}-UL2017_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9-v1`; UL18: `Run2018{A,B,C,D}-UL2018_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9-vN`. The `JMENanoAODv9` variants were not chosen.
- **DY families written (one MC config each, every config also holds all non-DY backgrounds mirroring the committed UL18 taxonomy):** `lo_inc`, `nlo_inc`, `lo_ht` (as in the repo) plus new `nlo_mll` (amcatnloFXFX M-XtoY bins), `nlo_ptll` (LHEFilterPtZ bins), `nlo_njet` (0J/1J/2J amcatnloFXFX), `nlo_njet_mll` (0J/1J/2J + the 24 `_MLL_` high-mass bins), `lo_njet` (madgraphMLM inclusive + DY1-4Jets). Family contents are in section 2; stitching caveats in section 8.
- **Cross sections:** taken, in priority order, from the committed UL18 configs, the AN-19-083 tables (scratchpad `an.txt` Tab. 6-8), and public references (uhh-cms/cmsdb, Latinos `samplesCrossSections_UL.py`). The CMS twikis (`StandardModelCrossSectionsat13TeV`, `SummaryTable1G25ns`) redirect to CERN SSO and could not be read; XSDB and McM GEN requests likewise need SSO. **One deliberate deviation from the committed UL18 configs:** the DY inclusive (LO and NLO) xsec is written as 6077.22 pb (AN/cmsdb NNLO) instead of 2025.74 pb, because 2025.74 = 6077.22/3 exactly (a per-lepton-flavour number applied to the three-flavour sample) and cmsdb quotes the same pdf uncertainty 14.78 that the committed config carries as `total_uncertainty`. All other UL18 values are kept even where references disagree (every disagreement is listed in section 5).
- **Unresolved cross sections (written as `null`):** the NLO mass-binned `DYJetsToLL_M-XtoY` amcatnloFXFX bins, the 24 `DYJetsToLL_{0,1,2}J_MLL_*` bins, `DY{1-4}JetsToLL_M-10to50_MatchEWPDG20`, and all official signal points except the 8 that coincide with the private UL18 grid. McM NanoAOD prepids are given in section 9 so they can be looked up with SSO (XSDB or McM chain -> wmLHEGEN request).
- **Signal:** official UL NanoAODv9 samples exist for all four eras, per flavour: `WRtoNEltoElElJJ_MWR{M}_N{N}` and `WRtoNMutoMuMuJJ_MWR{M}_N{N}` (madgraph-pythia8, TuneCP5), 105 mass points each (MWR = 1000, 2000, 4000, 6000, 8000; N = 100, 300, ..., MWR-100). Draft `<era>_signal.json` files list them. The committed UL18 signal config uses private `WRtoNLtoLLJJ_MWR*_MN*` samples (490 points, MWR 600-6000 in 200 GeV steps) and was NOT touched; only 8 mass points overlap.
- **Draft vs committed UL18:** the UL18 drafts differ from the committed configs only by (a) added `_ext1`/`_ext2` entries for the LO DY inclusive, the eight `WJetsToLNu_HT` bins and the four tribosons, (b) the added low-mass `DYJetsToLL_M-10to50` partner in `lo_inc`/`nlo_inc`, (c) the DY inclusive xsec 2025.74 -> 6077.22, and (d) the UL18 data draft now points to the 15Feb2022 re-reco (`_GT36`) NanoAODv9 datasets for SingleMuon and EGamma instead of the standard ones (section 1.0).

- **Repo working-tree state (observed 12:48-12:55, not written by this agent):** copies of these drafts already sit under `data/configs/RunII/2018/RunIISummer20UL18/` (modified `_data`, `_mc_dy_lo_inc`, `_mc_dy_nlo_inc`, `_mc_dy_lo_ht`, `_signal`; untracked `_mc_dy_{nlo_mll,nlo_ptll,nlo_njet,nlo_njet_mll,lo_njet}`). The `_data` (GT36 version), `_signal` and the five new-family copies are byte-identical to the drafts here; the three pre-existing families differ from the drafts only in field values (`mc_dy_lo_inc`: genEventSumw differs in 25 entries; `mc_dy_nlo_inc`: genEventSumw differs in 25 entries; `mc_dy_lo_ht`: genEventSumw differs in 32 entries), i.e. the copies carry filled `genEventSumw` and/or other edits made outside this agent. The committed private 490-point `RunIISummer20UL18_signal.json` therefore survives only in git HEAD (saved here as `../r2_private_signal_UL18_HEAD.json`).

## 1. Data

Trigger logic requires SingleMuon + SingleElectron + SinglePhoton (HLT_Photon175/200 in the electron OR) for 2016/2017 and SingleMuon + EGamma for 2018. 2016 is split at the HIPM boundary into the two eras below (preVFP/APV = Run2016B-ver1, B-ver2, C, D, E, F-HIPM, about 19.5 fb-1; postVFP = Run2016F, G, H, about 16.8 fb-1); both 2016B slices are included. Run2017G (5 TeV) and Run2017H (low pile-up; SingleMuon only on DBS) are excluded on purpose; 2018 = A-D.

Wildcards used: `/SingleMuon/Run2016*UL2016_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9*/NANOAOD`, `/SingleElectron/Run2016*UL2016_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9*/NANOAOD`, `/SinglePhoton/Run2016*UL2016_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9*/NANOAOD`, same for `Run2017*UL2017_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9*`, `/SingleMuon/Run2018*UL2018_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9*/NANOAOD`, `/EGamma/Run2018*UL2018_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9*/NANOAOD` (plus the broader `*NanoAODv9*`), and for the re-reco search `/{SingleMuon,EGamma,SingleElectron,SinglePhoton}/Run201*15Feb2022*/*`, `/.../Run201*Feb15*/*`, `/.../Run201*GT36*/*`, `/*/Run2016*GT36*/NANOAOD`, `/DoubleMuon/Run201*GT36*/NANOAOD`, `/MuonEG/Run201*GT36*/NANOAOD`.

### 1.0 The 15Feb2022 ReReReco (high-pT muon fix) = the `_GT36` NanoAODv9 datasets

PdmV: *for some of the datasets in 2018 and also 2017, an even newer-than-UL ReReReco version 15Feb2022 contains a fix for high-pT muons*. On DBS the 15Feb2022 re-reco exists as AOD/MINIAOD (`/SingleMuon/Run2017{B..H}-15Feb2022_UL2017-v1`, `/SingleMuon/Run2018{A..D}-15Feb2022_UL2018-v1`, `/EGamma/Run2018{A..D}-15Feb2022_UL2018-v1`; also DoubleMuon/MuonEG 2018) and its NanoAODv9 is the `*_UL201x_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9_GT36-vN` family: DBS parentage `/SingleMuon/Run2018A-UL2018_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9_GT36-v1/NANOAOD` <- `/SingleMuon/Run2018A-UL2018_MiniAODv2_GT36-v1/MINIAOD` <- `/SingleMuon/Run2018A-15Feb2022_UL2018-v1/AOD` (same for EGamma 2018 and SingleMuon 2017), whereas the standard `UL2018_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9-v2` descends from `12Nov2019_UL2018` AOD and the standard 2017 one from `09Aug2019_UL2017` AOD. The `UL2018Feb15_MiniAODv2_JMENanoAODv9` datasets are the JME-flavour NanoAOD of the same re-reco (not used). **No 15Feb2022/GT36 datasets exist for SingleElectron or SinglePhoton (2017) or for anything in 2016.**

Because this analysis selects high-pT muons, the drafts use the GT36 datasets wherever they exist and are complete (SingleMuon 2017C-F, SingleMuon 2018A-D, EGamma 2018A-D; NOT 2017B, see below) and keep the standard datasets as the documented alternative (the committed UL18 config uses the standard ones, so the UL18 data draft now differs from it). Lumi-section check (DBS `filelumis` per block, intersected with the legacy golden JSONs downloaded from the DQM certification site): the standard datasets contain every certified lumi section that the GT36 ones contain, while the GT36 re-reco is missing certified lumi sections in some cases. **SingleMuon Run2017B GT36 is incomplete: 6138 of 24280 certified lumi sections (25%, spread over many runs, matching its 102.07 M vs 136.30 M events) are missing, so the standard `/SingleMuon/Run2017B-UL2017_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9-v1/NANOAOD` is kept in the UL17 config and the GT36 one is only listed as an (incomplete) alternative.** The other GT36 datasets lose at most a few per mille of certified lumi sections (table above); they are used as requested, but the luminosity must then be computed with brilcalc on the processed lumi mask of exactly these datasets (or the missing lumi sections recovered from the standard datasets), and the 2017 muon channel will combine the re-reco SingleMuon with standard SingleElectron/SinglePhoton.

### 1.0b Golden JSONs and luminosity

Verified today on https://cms-service-dqmdc.web.cern.ch/CAF/certification/ : `Collisions16/13TeV/Legacy_2016/Cert_271036-284044_13TeV_Legacy2016_Collisions16_JSON.txt` (and `..._MuonPhys.txt`), `Collisions17/13TeV/Legacy_2017/Cert_294927-306462_13TeV_UL2017_Collisions17_GoldenJSON.txt` (and `..._MuonJSON.txt`), `Collisions18/13TeV/Legacy_2018/Cert_314472-325175_13TeV_Legacy2018_Collisions18_JSON.txt` (and `..._MuonPhys.txt`; the 2018 one is already in the repo under `data/lumis/RunII/2018/RunIISummer20UL18/`). Recommended integrated luminosities (LumiRecommendationsRun2, Golden JSON, normtag PHYSICS, as provided by the coordinator - the twiki itself is behind CERN SSO so the `Legacy` row could not be read): 2016 36.31 fb-1 (preVFP about 19.5 + postVFP about 16.8), 2017 41.48 fb-1, 2018 59.83 fb-1 (total 137.62). Older values used in AN-19-083 Tab. 3: 35.92 / 41.53 / 59.97 fb-1 (59.74 in the figure labels), total 137.4.

Nominal era boundaries (CMS run registry): 2016B 272007-275376 (ver1 = the small early slice 272007-273017, ver2 = 273150-275376; both are needed), C 275657-276283, D 276315-276811, E 276831-277420, F 277772-278808 (preVFP/HIPM up to 278801, postVFP from 278802), G 278820-280385, H 280919-284044; 2017B 297046-299329, C 299368-302029, D 302030-303434, E 303824-304797, F 305040-306462; 2018A 315252-316995, B 316998-319312, C 319313-320393, D 320394-325273 (AN-19-083 Tab. 3). The DBS `runs` min/max below are what the datasets actually contain.

**Run2016F HIPM / non-HIPM split check:** the two 2016F datasets overlap in their run *ranges* (277932-278807 vs 278769-278808) but their run *lists* are disjoint for all three PDs: HIPM holds 31 runs including 278770, 278806, 278807; non-HIPM holds 7 runs: 278769, 278801-278805, 278808. So the split is not a clean run-number cut (runs 278806/278807 sit in HIPM although > 278801) but there is no double counting at run level; both datasets must be kept, each in its own era with its own lumi mask.

Naming convention used for `dataset`: `<PD>_Run<year><period>` as in the committed UL18 config, with `_ver1`/`_ver2` appended for the two 2016B slices; the Run2016F datasets carry the same `dataset` name (`<PD>_Run2016F`) in the two 2016 eras, so any output keyed only on `dataset` must also carry the era.

Other alternatives seen and not chosen: `*_JMENanoAODv9-v1` and `UL2018Feb15_MiniAODv2_JMENanoAODv9` (JME flavour); `/SingleMuon/Run2017{G,H}-UL2017_MiniAODv2_NanoAODv9[_GT36]-v1/NANOAOD` (special runs); the `UL201x_MiniAODv1_NanoAODv2_GT36` NanoAODv2 of the re-reco. Lower versions do not exist for the chosen data datasets except `/SinglePhoton/Run2016C-HIPM_...-v4` (v2/v3 are not VALID) and `/SingleMuon/Run2017G-..._GT36-v1` (excluded era).

## 2. DY variant families

- **`lo_inc`** - madgraphMLM inclusive: `DYJetsToLL_M-50` (+`_ext1` in UL17/UL18) and the low-mass partner `DYJetsToLL_M-10to50`.
- **`nlo_inc`** - amcatnloFXFX inclusive: `DYJetsToLL_M-50` and `DYJetsToLL_M-10to50`.
- **`lo_ht`** - madgraphMLM `DYJetsToLL_M-50_HT-{70to100,...,2500toInf}_TuneCP5_PSweights` (8 bins; UL16APV/UL16 are `-v2`, UL17/UL18 `-v1`). No inclusive sample in the family (HT<70 not covered, as in the committed UL18 config).
- **`nlo_mll`** - amcatnloFXFX mass-binned: `DYJetsToLL_M-{100to200,200to500,500to700,700to800,800to1000,1000to1500,1500to2000,2000to3000}` plus the inclusive `M-50` and `M-10to50` samples for mll < 100 GeV (the inclusive sample must be truncated at mll = 100 downstream). No `M-3000toInf` bin exists on DAS. **xsec unresolved** for the eight bins.
- **`nlo_ptll`** - amcatnloFXFX `DYJetsToLL_LHEFilterPtZ-{0To50,50To100,100To250,250To400,400To650,650ToInf}_MatchEWPDG20` (complete in LHE pT(Z), mll > 50). The pre-UL `DYJetsToLL_Pt-*` samples do not exist in UL.
- **`nlo_njet`** - amcatnloFXFX `DYJetsToLL_{0J,1J,2J}` (complete in LHE nJ, mll > 50).
- **`nlo_njet_mll`** - the `nlo_njet` samples plus `DYJetsToLL_{0J,1J,2J}_MLL_{200_400,400_800,800_1400,1400_2300,2300_3500,3500_4500,4500_6000,6000_Inf}` (24 high-mass bins). The 0J/1J/2J inclusive samples must be truncated at mll = 200 downstream. **xsec unresolved** for the 24 bins.
- **`lo_njet`** - madgraphMLM `DYJetsToLL_M-50` (+ext1) with `DY{1,2,3,4}JetsToLL_M-50_MatchEWPDG20`, and `DYJetsToLL_M-10to50` with `DY{1,2,3,4}JetsToLL_M-10to50_MatchEWPDG20` (+`_ext1` for DY1-3Jets). Requires LHE-nJet stitching of the inclusive sample; note the jet-binned samples use the `MatchEWPDG20` generator settings while the inclusive sample does not. **xsec unresolved** for the four M-10to50 jet bins.

Wildcards used for the DY sweep (per campaign `{c}` in `RunIISummer20UL16NanoAODAPVv9*`, `RunIISummer20UL16NanoAODv9*`, `RunIISummer20UL17NanoAODv9*`, `RunIISummer20UL18NanoAODv9*`): `/DYJetsTo*/{c}/NANOAODSIM`, `/DYToLL*/{c}/NANOAODSIM`, `/DY*JetsToLL*/{c}/NANOAODSIM`, `/DY{1,2,3,4}JetsToLL*/{c}/NANOAODSIM`, `/DYJetsToEE*/{c}/NANOAODSIM`, `/DYJetsToMuMu*/{c}/NANOAODSIM`, `/ZToEE*/{c}/NANOAODSIM`, `/ZToMuMu*/{c}/NANOAODSIM`; tau-only datasets dropped by name. Non-DY wildcards: `/TTTo2L2Nu*/`, `/TTToSemiLeptonic*/`, `/TTToHadronic*/`, `/TTJets_TuneCP5*/`, `/ST_tW_top*/`, `/ST_tW_antitop*/`, `/ST_t-channel_top*/`, `/ST_t-channel_antitop*/`, `/ST_s-channel*/`, `/WJetsToLNu*/`, `/W{1-4}JetsToLNu*/`, `/WW_Tune*/`, `/WZ_Tune*/`, `/ZZ_Tune*/`, `/WWTo*/`, `/WZTo*/`, `/ZZTo*/`, `/WWW*/`, `/WWZ*/`, `/WZZ*/`, `/ZZZ*/`, `/ttZJets*/`, `/ttWJets*/`, `/TTZToLLNuNu*/`, `/TTZToQQ*/`, `/TTWJetsToLNu*/`, `/TTWJetsToQQ*/`, `/ttHTobb*/`, `/ttHToNonbb*/`, `/TTZToLL_M*/`, `/TTGJets*/`, `/tZq*/` (each with `/{c}/NANOAODSIM`).

Non-DY process list mirrors the committed UL18 configs exactly (no TTToHadronic, no TTZToLLNuNu/TTWJetsToLNu, no ttH): tt_tW = TTTo2L2Nu, ST_tW_top/antitop 5f NoFullyHadronicDecays; Nonprompt = WJetsToLNu_HT x8, ST_s-channel leptonDecays, ST_t-channel top/antitop 5f InclusiveDecays, TTToSemiLeptonic; Other = ttZJets, ttWJets (madgraphMLM), WW, WZ, ZZ (pythia8), WWW_4F, WWZ_4F, WZZ, ZZZ (amcatnlo). The same primary datasets exist in all four eras (only versions differ).

## 3. MC datasets per era

One row per unique dataset; the `families` column says which DY config(s) carry it (`all` = every MC config of the era). Sizes are decimal GB from DBS `file_size`.

## 4. Signal

Wildcards used (all campaigns, then filtered to the standard UL conditions): `/WRtoNLtoLLJJ*/*/NANOAODSIM`, `/WRToNLToLLJJ*/*/NANOAODSIM`, `/WR*ToLNu*/*/NANOAODSIM`, `/WRtoNEl*/`, `/WRtoNMu*/`, `/WRtoNE*/`, `/WRtoNM*/`, `/WRToNE*/`, `/WRToNMu*/`, `/WR_*/`, `/WR*MWR*/`, `/WR*MW-*/`, `/*WRtoNL*/`, `/*WR_M*/`, `/WRtoNL*/`, `/WRtoN*/`, `/WRToN*/`, `/WR*/*UL*/`, `/*WRto*/*UL*/`, `/*WRTo*/*UL*/`, `/WR*/*/`, `/*_WR_*/`, `/*HeavyNeutrino*/*UL*/` (8789 distinct datasets in total, 3868 in Summer20UL campaigns).

What exists in the four standard UL NanoAODv9 campaigns:

- `WRtoNEltoElElJJ_MWR{M}_N{N}_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8` and `WRtoNMutoMuMuJJ_MWR{M}_N{N}_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8`: **105 mass points per flavour in every era** (MWR = 1000, 2000, 4000, 6000, 8000 GeV; N from 100 to MWR-100 in 200 GeV steps). Mostly `-v2`; a handful of primaries only exist as `-v3` (UL16APV: 2, UL16: 3, UL17: 4, UL18: 1). EXO-group requests (e.g. `EXO-RunIISummer20UL18NanoAODv9-06506` for ee MWR1000_N100).
- `WRtoNTautoTauTauJJ_WR*_N*` (~437 per era) and `WRtoTauNtoTauTauJets_WR*_N*` (~90 per era, with `_ext1`): tau channel, not needed.
- `WRtoNLtoLLJJ_*` (the old combined ee+mumu naming) exists ONLY in pre-UL campaigns (RunIISummer16NanoAODv5-7 `WR*_N*` points, and `MWR500to3500/3500to5000/5000to6000` rpscan samples in Summer16/Fall17/Autumn18 NanoAODv7); nothing in any UL campaign. `HeavyNeutrino_trilepton_*` (type-I seesaw) also exists in UL but is a different model.
- Pre-UL campaigns seen for completeness: RunIISummer16NanoAOD(v3-v7), RunIIFall17NanoAOD(v4-v7), RunIIAutumn18NanoAOD(v4-v7), Run3Summer22/22EE/23/23BPix NanoAODv12 (~638 each).

Comparison with the committed private UL18 grid (`WRtoNLtoLLJJ_MWR{M}_MN{N}`, 490 points, MWR 600-6000 step 200, N in {100, 200, 400, 600, ..., MWR-100}): only 8 points coincide with the official grid: (1000,100), (1000,900), (2000,100), (2000,1900), (4000,100), (4000,3900), (6000,100), (6000,5900). The official grid adds MWR = 8000 and odd-hundred N values; the private grid is much denser for MWR <= 6000.

**Cross sections for the official samples:** the private config values were verified to be exactly AN-19-083 LO x k(NLO/LO) from App. A Tab. 23-41 for all 490 points (parsed into `r2_an_signal_xsec.json`, 664 points, MWR 200-7000). The AN states the ee and mumu channels have identical cross sections, so for the 8 overlapping points the per-flavour draft entries carry **(private ee+mumu value)/2**; all other 97 points per flavour are `null` (XSDB/McM need SSO; GenXSecAnalyzer on one file per point is the fallback). Overlap values:

| point | AN LO [pb] | k | private (ee+mumu) | per-flavour draft |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| MWR1000 N100 | 6.383 | 1.21 | 7.72343 | 3.86171 |
| MWR1000 N900 | 0.4125 | 1.22 | 0.50325 | 0.251625 |
| MWR2000 N100 | 0.2622 | 1.29 | 0.338238 | 0.169119 |
| MWR2000 N1900 | 0.005147 | 1.31 | 0.00674257 | 0.00337129 |
| MWR4000 N100 | 0.003258 | 1.34 | 0.00436572 | 0.00218286 |
| MWR4000 N3900 | 1.456e-05 | 1.42 | 2.06752e-05 | 1.03376e-05 |
| MWR6000 N100 | 0.0002783 | 1.27 | 0.000353441 | 0.00017672 |
| MWR6000 N5900 | 2.731e-07 | 1.44 | 3.93264e-07 | 1.96632e-07 |

- **RunIISummer20UL16APV** - `RunIISummer20UL16APV_signal.json`: 210 datasets (105 ee + 105 mumu), 1718 files, 7.29 M events, 27 GB. `-v3` primaries: WRtoNEltoElElJJ_MWR8000_N5900_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8, WRtoNMutoMuMuJJ_MWR8000_N100_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8. Alternatives (other VALID versions): 0.
- **RunIISummer20UL16** - `RunIISummer20UL16_signal.json`: 210 datasets (105 ee + 105 mumu), 1677 files, 7.30 M events, 27 GB. `-v3` primaries: WRtoNEltoElElJJ_MWR8000_N500_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8, WRtoNMutoMuMuJJ_MWR2000_N1100_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8, WRtoNMutoMuMuJJ_MWR8000_N5500_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8. Alternatives (other VALID versions): 0.
- **RunIISummer20UL17** - `RunIISummer20UL17_signal.json`: 210 datasets (105 ee + 105 mumu), 2231 files, 14.56 M events, 52 GB. `-v3` primaries: WRtoNEltoElElJJ_MWR8000_N1300_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8, WRtoNMutoMuMuJJ_MWR4000_N2500_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8, WRtoNMutoMuMuJJ_MWR8000_N3500_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8, WRtoNMutoMuMuJJ_MWR8000_N4300_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8. Alternatives (other VALID versions): 0.
- **RunIISummer20UL18** - `RunIISummer20UL18_signal.json`: 210 datasets (105 ee + 105 mumu), 2167 files, 14.57 M events, 52 GB. `-v3` primaries: WRtoNMutoMuMuJJ_MWR8000_N4700_TuneCP5_13TeV_madgraph-pythia8. Alternatives (other VALID versions): 0.

Per-point file summaries are in Appendix A.

## 5. Cross-section table and discrepancies

Values are 13 TeV and era-independent (same number in all four drafts). Sources: `UL18 config` = committed `RunIISummer20UL18_mc_dy_*.json`; `AN` = AN-19-083 Tab. 6/7/8 (scratchpad `an.txt` lines 358-500); `cmsdb` = uhh-cms/cmsdb `processes/ewk.py` + `processes/top.py` (fetched today); `Latinos` = latinos/LatinoAnalysis `NanoGardener/python/framework/samples/samplesCrossSections_UL.py` (branch UL_production; ref E = SM twiki, W = XSDB). The twikis themselves were not readable (SSO).

Further cross-checks from cmsdb/Latinos not in the table: ttbar inclusive 833.9 pb (cmsdb, NNLO+NNLL) vs 831.76 (Latinos `TTJets-LO`, same twiki older value) - the committed TTTo2L2Nu 88.56 corresponds to 833.9 x 0.1062 while TTToSemiLeptonic 365.34 corresponds to 831.76 x 0.4392 (two different ttbar totals inside one config); single-top t-channel NNLO 134.2/80.0 (cmsdb) vs NLO 136.02/80.95 used; tW NNLO 79.3 total (cmsdb) vs the 35.85 x BR convention behind 19.2/19.56; s-channel 11.073 (cmsdb) x BR(W->lnu)=0.3258 would give 3.61 vs 3.36 used; DY `M-4to50_HT-{100to200,200to400,400to600,600toInf}` XSDB (Latinos) 204.0/54.39/5.697/1.85 if the low-mass HT partners are ever wanted.

Unverified ballpark numbers (from memory of the 2016 SummaryTable1G25ns for the pre-UL amcatnloFXFX mass bins; **do not use without XSDB confirmation**): M-100to200 226.6, M-500to700 0.2334, M-700to800 0.03614, M-800to1000 0.03047, M-1000to1500 0.01636, M-1500to2000 0.00218, M-2000to3000 0.0005156 pb; the UL `M-200to500` bin has no pre-UL counterpart.

## 6. Version choices and alternatives

| era | dataset chosen | other VALID standard-condition versions (not chosen) |
|---|---|---|

No chosen dataset has a second VALID standard-condition version, i.e. the table above is empty by construction (the only multi-version cases on DBS are different `_extN` tags, which are all kept).
Primary datasets (with ext tag) whose `-vN` differs between eras (same physics, different production version): `DY1JetsToLL_M-10to50_MatchEWPDG20_TuneCP5_13TeV-madgraphMLM-pythia8` (RunIISummer20UL16: v1, RunIISummer20UL16APV: v1, RunIISummer20UL17: v2, RunIISummer20UL18: v2); ## 7. Other DY -> ll variants on DAS that were NOT put in a config

All exist in the four standard campaigns unless noted. Listed so nothing is silently dropped:

- `DYJetsToEE_M-*_H2ErratumFix_TuneCP5_13TeV-powhegMiNNLO-pythia8-photos` and `DYJetsToMuMu_M-*_H2ErratumFix_...` with M-10to50 (+ext1), 100to200, 200to400, 400to500, 500to700, 700to800, 800to1000, 1000to1500, 1500to2000, 2000toInf, plus the M-50 pieces `DYJetsToEE_M-50_massWgtFix` (all eras), `DYJetsToMuMu_M-50_massWgtFix` (UL17/UL18 only), `DYJetsToMuMu_M-50_TuneCP5_13TeV-powhegMiNNLO` (UL16APV/UL16 only), `DYJetsTo{EE,MuMu}_M-50_TuneCP5_ZptWeighted` (all), `DYJetsToMuMu_H2ErratumFix[_PDFExt]` (inclusive). This per-flavour MiNNLO mass-binned set is the closest Run 2 analogue of the Run 3 `nlo_mll` family (powheg `DYto2E/DYto2Mu_MLL-*`); not configured because the M-50 piece is inconsistent across eras and no public xsec source was found.
- `ZToMuMu_M-{50To120,120To200,200To400,400To800,800To1400,1400To2300,2300To3500,3500To4500,4500To6000,6000ToInf}_TuneCP5_13TeV-powheg-pythia8` (+`_ext1` for the first six): muon channel only, no `ZToEE` counterpart in UL NanoAODv9.
- `DYJetsToMuMu_M-50_TuneCP5_13TeV-amcatnloFXFX-pythia8` (all eras), `DYJetsToEE_M-100To160[_VBFFilter]_TuneCP5_13TeV-amcatnloFXFX-pythia8` (UL16/17/18): single-flavour partial sets.
- `DYJetsToLL_M-4to50_HT-{70to100,100to200,200to400,400to600,600toInf}_TuneCP5_13TeV-madgraphMLM-pythia8`: low-mass HT partner of `lo_ht` (XSDB values in section 5).
- `DYJetsToLL_M-50_Zpt-{100to200,200toInf}_BPSFilter_TuneCP5_13TeV-madgraphMLM-pythia8` (200toInf missing in UL16APV) and `DYBJetsToLL_M-50_Zpt-{100to200,200toInf}`: Z-pT-binned, b-parton-shower-filtered set for DY+b studies.
- `DYJetsToLL_M-50_TuneCH3_13TeV-madgraphMLM-herwig7` (all eras) and `DYToLL_NLO_5FS_TuneCH3_13TeV_matchbox_herwig7` (UL18 only, +ext1): alternative-shower samples.
- Tau-only (`DYJetsToTauTau*`, `WRtoNTau*`) and `DYJetsToLL_M-50_TuneCP5_13TeV-madgraphMLM-pythia8` non-standard variants (JMENano, FlatPU, FSUL) are excluded by construction.

## 8. Overlap / stitching concerns (not solved here)

- `lo_ht`: no HT < 70 GeV coverage (same as the committed UL18 config). If the inclusive `M-50` sample is added for the low-HT slice, it must be restricted to LHE HT < 70 GeV downstream.
- `nlo_mll` / `nlo_njet_mll`: the inclusive samples (`M-50` resp. `0J/1J/2J`) overlap with the mass bins above mll = 100 resp. 200 GeV and must be truncated with the LHE dilepton mass; the `M-10to50` sample overlaps with nothing (mll < 50).
- `lo_njet`: the inclusive `DYJetsToLL_M-50` (and `M-10to50`) must be restricted to LHE nJ = 0 (or stitched with the standard nJ-weight method); the jet-binned samples carry different generator settings (`MatchEWPDG20`) than the inclusive one.
- `nlo_ptll`: complete in LHE pT(Z) so no inclusive sample is needed, but the cmsdb 0To50 cross section (1494 pb) makes the family sum ~1994 pb, far below the 6077 pb inclusive NNLO value; verify before use or stitch the inclusive sample for pT(Z) < 50.
- `nlo_inc`/`lo_inc`: the `M-10to50` partner starts at mll = 10 GeV and `M-50` at 50 GeV, no overlap.
- The HT-binned DY values are bare LO while the inclusive samples are NNLO-normalised (see memory note `ul18-dy-xsec-open-items`); the W+jets HT values are 2016-era LO x 1.21; the normalisation convention is therefore mixed across families.

   Official signal points: EXO-RunIISummer20UL1x NanoAODv9 requests (e.g. `EXO-RunIISummer20UL18NanoAODv9-06506` = ee MWR1000_N100); GenXSecAnalyzer on one file per point is the SSO-free alternative.
2. **DY inclusive xsec**: confirm the 6077.22 choice (and fix the committed UL18 `lo_inc`/`nlo_inc` configs, which carry 6077.22/3 = 2025.74).
3. **Cross-section convention**: W+jets HT bins, tribosons, ttW/ttZ and TTTo2L2Nu use 2016-era / LO / mixed-ttbar-total values (section 5); decide whether to move all eras to the UL XSDB values at once.
4. **`nlo_mll` naming**: in Run 3 `nlo_mll` means per-flavour powheg mass bins; here it means amcatnloFXFX `DYJetsToLL_M-XtoY`. Rename (e.g. `nlo_mll_fxfx`) if the two should not share a `--dy` name, and register the new variants in `wrcoffea/config.yaml` (`dy_variants`, `default_mc_tag`).
5. **Golden JSONs and other per-era inputs** (`lumi_jsons`, JERC, pileup, SF payloads in `config.yaml`) do not exist yet for UL16APV/UL16/UL17; the data drafts need `Cert_271036-284044_13TeV_Legacy2016_Collisions16_JSON.txt` (both 2016 eras) and `Cert_294927-306462_13TeV_UL2017_Collisions17_GoldenJSON.txt` (names verified on the DQM certification site, section 1.0b).
5b. **GT36 re-reco completeness**: SingleMuon Run2017B GT36 is 25% incomplete and is not used; SingleMuon 2017F (0.5%), EGamma 2018D (0.16%) and a few others lose per-mille-level certified lumi sections (section 1.0 table) - compute the luminosity with brilcalc on the processed lumi mask of the chosen datasets rather than quoting the golden-JSON totals. Also decide whether mixing re-reco SingleMuon with standard SingleElectron/SinglePhoton in 2017 is acceptable (different PDs, so no event overlap issue, but different reco for the muon-channel and electron-channel data).
6. **2016F**: keep both F datasets (one per era); the run-level split is verified disjoint but should also be checked at lumi-section level against the golden JSON once the lumi masks are in place.
7. **`dataset` naming for extensions** (`<primary>_ext1`) is a convention introduced here; adjust if the skimmer/fileset scripts key on the exact primary name.
8. **Signal per-flavour xsec** = (combined private value)/2 assumes equal ee and mumu branching (stated in the AN); the official samples should be cross-checked with GenXSecAnalyzer.
